                                                                      CHAIRMAN’S STATEMENT
                                                                                    主席報告

In 2025, Haichang Ocean Park completed the most significant            2025 年，海昌海洋公園完成了發展歷程
strategic reorganization in its development history. As the newly      中最重要的一次戰略重組。作為新任主
appointed Chairman, I am deeply honored by this mission and            席，深感使命光榮、責任重大，本人謹
mindful of the great responsibility it entails. On behalf of the       欣然代表本公司董事（「董事」）會（「董事
board (the “Board”) of the directors (the “Director(s)”) of the        會」）向本公司各位股東（「股東」）提呈我
Company, I am pleased to present to the shareholders of the            們截至 2025 年 12 月 31 日止年度（「報告
Company (the “Shareholders”) the review of our annual results          期」）的年度業績回顧及展望。
for the year ended 31 December 2025 (the “Reporting Period”)
and our prospect.


WHERE RIVERS MEET THE OCEAN:                                           山海相逢，開啟新征程
NAVIGATING A NEW COURSE
During the period, we introduced a new controlling shareholder.        期內，我們引入新的控股股東，通過
Through a capital injection of HK$2,295 million, Sunriver Starrysea    22.95 億港元的注資，祥源星海旅遊（開
Tourism (Cayman) Co., Ltd. officially became our controlling           曼）有限公司以持股38.60%正式成為我
shareholder with a 38.60% equity interest. Upon completion             們的控股股東。隨着認購事項的完成，
of the subscription, the corporate governance structure of the         公司治理結構同步調整。公司在保持戰
Company was adjusted accordingly. While maintaining the                略、業務及核心團隊穩定性的同時，
stability of its strategy, business and core team, the Company has     借助雙方產業資源優勢，持續強化管理
leveraged the industrial resources of both parties to continuously     運營，優化並升級既定的發展戰略。此
strengthen its management and operations, as well as to optimize       番股權重組，我們收穫的不僅是資金支
and upgrade its established development strategies. Through this       持，更是資源賦能及產業能力的提升，
shareholding restructuring, we have gained not only financial          強強聯合構建文旅市場新版圖。
support but also resource empowerment and enhanced industrial
capabilities, forming a powerful alliance to build a new landscape
in the cultural tourism market.


ADHERING TO ESTABLISHED DEVELOPMENT                                    堅持既定的發展戰略，三輪驅
STRATEGIES: THREE-WHEEL DRIVE AND FULL-                                動，全鏈賦能
CHAIN EMPOWERMENT
At present, our development strategy has become clearer and            當前，我們的發展戰略更加清晰堅定。
more resolute. Leveraging a solid moat built over two decades          依托二十餘年來在生物保育、主題公園
in biological conservation, theme park operations and brand            運營、品牌建設等方面積累的深厚護城
building, the Company has established a progressive system with        河，以主題公園運營為根基、以OAAS
theme park operations as the bedrock, OAAS (Operation as a             （運營即服務）為槓桿、以 IP 運營為飛輪
Service) as the lever, IP operations as the flywheel and “biology      的遞進體系、以「生物 + 科技」為新的增
+ technology” as the new driving force. We will continue to            長 點 ， 我 們 將 持 續 深 化「 主 題 公 園 運 營
deepen our “Theme Park Operations + OAAS + IP Operations”              +OAAS+IP運營」三輪驅動的商業模式，
three-wheel drive business model, while constantly extending           同時，不斷拓寬業務模式，創新研發生
our business model, pioneering in the development of products          物科技產品，並探索新興市場化應用，
featuring biotechnology, exploring emerging market-oriented            踐行ESG理念，成為海洋主題文旅全產業
applications and practicing our ESG philosophy, to position            鏈的賦能者。
ourselves as an enabler across the entire industrial chain of
oceanic culture-based tourism.

    acquisition, investment, construction and capital expenditure,       為長期業績增長注入動力，還降低投資
    while Haichang shall be responsible for project operation and        風險，提升單體公園經營利潤。
    would consolidate the revenue from the project into its financial
    statements. This approach not only injects momentum into long-
    term performance growth but also mitigates investment risks and
    enhances the operating profit of individual parks.

4.   Strategy and Outlook                                            4.   戰略與未來展望
     Currently, we are progressively introducing various resources        目前，我們逐步引入有利於集團發
     conducive to the Group’s development, striving to enhance            展的各類資源，努力提高運營效率
     operational efficiency and quality, and improving the                與質量，改善集團的資產負債狀
     Group’s gearing position to achieve new quality growth               況，以實現三大業務的新質增長。
     across our three major business segments.


     For our park assets, we focus on enhancing quality and               對於公園資產，我們對存量的優質
     efficiency of existing premium parks to improve operational          公園提質增效，提高運營效率，擴
     efficiency, thereby expanding revenue and profit scales              大營收與利潤規模，使其成為優質
     and transforming them into high-quality cash-flow                    的現金流資產。對於增量公園項
     generating assets. For our incremental park projects, we are         目，我們積極推進按期開業，釋放
     actively promoting timely openings to unlock new growth              新增長動能。對於低效資產，我們
     momentum. For underperforming assets, we will endeavor               將努力調整經營模式，積極引入戰
     to adjust our business models, actively introduce strategic          略合作夥伴，調整適應市場的內容
     partners, realign our contents and products to adapt to              產品，提高資產使用效率，以回籠
     the market, and enhance our asset utilization efficiency to          資金，降低負債。
     recover capital and reduce our debt.


     In the future, we will fully pivot towards an asset-                 我們未來將全力轉向輕資產服務經
     light service and operation model to avoid heavy-asset               營模式，避免重資產投入，爭取重
     investments. We strive to achieve future expansion of our            點通過以文旅服務和解決方案輸出
     tourism & leisure assets primarily through Operation as              (OAAS)的方式來實現未來的文旅資
     a Service (OAAS), which focuses on delivery of tourism               產擴張，聚焦1000萬人口級城市，
     & leisure services and solutions. Focusing on cities with            打造「輕資產 + 生物科普展示 + 運營
     populations of over 10 million, we will develop an                   服務」一體化輕資產輸出模式，以北
     integrated asset-light delivery model characterized by               京公園等項目為樣板，打磨標準化
     “asset-light + biological science popularization display +           輸出體系，形成可複製、規模化的
     operation services”. Taking projects such as Beijing Park            全國佈局；同時，深化各地商業合
     as a benchmark, we will refine our standardized delivery             作，加強公司品牌價值輸出，同步
     system to form a replicable and large-scale national layout.         賦能公司項目運營，該等輕資產的
     Meanwhile, we will deepen commercial cooperations across             輸出方式將我們的內部能力轉化為
     various regions and enhance the delivery of the Company’s            外部營收，且將有效避免大量的自
     brand value to simultaneously empower the Company’s                  有資本支出。
     project operations. This asset-light delivery model converts
     our internal capabilities into external revenue and will
     effectively avoid significant self-owned capital expenditure.

experience.

SHARE OPTION SCHEME                                                   購股權計劃
2024 Share Option Scheme                                              2024年購股權計劃
On 28 June 2024, the Company adopted a share option scheme            於2024 年6 月28 日，本公司採納一項購
(the “2024 Share Option Scheme”). The purposes of the 2024            股權計劃（「2024年購股權計劃」）。2024
Share Option Scheme are (i) to recognise the contributions by         年購股權計劃旨在(i)認可合資格參與者作
eligible participants by providing them with an opportunity to        出之貢獻，為彼等提供獲得本公司所有
acquire a proprietary interest in the Company; (ii) to encourage      權權益的機會；(ii) 為本集團的持續經營
and retain such individuals for the continual operation and           及發展鼓勵及留住有關人士，並為彼等
development of the Group, as well as to provide additional            實現表現目標提供額外獎勵；及(iii)為本
incentives for them to achieve performance goals; and (iii)           公司提供靈活方法，以挽留、獎勵、回
to provide the Company with a flexible means of retaining,            報、酬勞、補償合資格參與者及 ╱ 或向
incentivising, rewarding, remunerating, compensating and/or           合資格參與者提供福利。2024 年購股權
providing benefits to eligible participants. The 2024 Share Option    計劃的有效期為自採納日期起十年。
Scheme shall be valid and effective for a period of ten years
commencing on the adoption date.

     NOMINATION, APPOINTMENT, RETIREMENT                                     提名、委任、退任及重選
     AND RE-ELECTION
     In accordance with the Articles of Association, at least one-third      根據章程細則，當時為數三分之一的董
     of the Directors for the time being (or, if their number is not three   事（如董事人數並非三或三的倍數，則須
     or a multiple of three, then the number nearest to, but not less        為最接近但不少於三分之一的董事人數）
     than, one-third) shall retire from office by rotation and shall be      須輪值退任，並合資格於各股東週年大
     eligible for re-election and re-appointment at every annual general     會上重選或連任，惟各董事須最少每三
     meeting provided that each Director shall be subject to retirement      年輪值退任一次，而任何因填補臨時職
     by rotation at least once every three years and any new Director        位空缺或作為董事會新增成員而獲委任
     appointed to fill a causal vacancy or as an addition to the Board       的新任董事須在接受委任後之下一次股
     shall submit himself/herself for re-election by Shareholders at the     東大會上由股東進行重選。
     next general meeting after appointment.


     BOARD MEETINGS                                                          董事會會議
     The Board intends to hold Board meetings regularly at least             董事會擬定期舉行董事會會議，即每年
     four times a year at approximately quarterly intervals which are        至少舉行四次會議及大致按季度舉行會
     normally scheduled in the fourth quarter of the preceding year.         議，一般會於第四季預定會議時間。董
     Notices of not less than fourteen days will be given for all regular    事會常規會議通知將於會議舉行前至少
     Board meetings to provide all Directors with an opportunity             十四日送呈全體董事，讓其安排出席會
     to attend and include matters in the agenda for regular Board           議，並於常規董事會會議議程內載入所
     meetings. The Board will also hold meeting on other occasions           要討論的事宜。董事會亦會於某事項需
     when a board-level decision on a particular matter is required.         要作出董事會決議時舉行會議，並會發
     Reasonable notice will generally be given.                              出合理通知。

ANTI-CORRUPTION POLICY                                                  反貪污政策
The Group does not tolerate any form of bribery, whether direct         本公司絕不容忍其董事、高級職員、僱
or indirect, by, or of, its Directors, officers, employees, agents or   員、代理或顧問或為其行事或為彼等代
consultants or any persons or companies acting for it or on its         表之任何人士或公司，直接或間接地作
behalf. The Company adopts the anti-corruption policy to assist         出任何形式之賄賂。本公司採用反貪污
employees in recognising circumstance which may lead to or              政策以幫助僱員識別可能導致或可能被
give the appearance of being involved in corruption or unethical        牽涉入賄賂及不道德商業行為之情況，
business conduct, so as to avoid such conduct which is clearly          從而避免作出該等屬明確禁止之行為，
prohibited, and to promptly seek guidance where necessary.              並於必要時迅速尋求指引。

We will review the anti-corruption policy on a regular basis            我們將定期檢討反貪污政策，任何被定
and report any sentenced cases to the legal and compliance              罪的案件將向本公司的法律及合規部門
department of the Company.                                              匯報。

WHISTLEBLOWING POLICY                                                   舉報政策
The Company expects and encourages employees of the Group               本公司期望並鼓勵本集團僱員以及與本
and those who deal with the Group (e.g. suppliers, customers,           集團有業務往來之人士（例如供應商、客
creditors and debtors) to report to the Company, in confidence,         戶 、 債 權 人 及 債 務 人 ）以 保 密 方 式 向 本
any suspected impropriety, misconduct or malpractice concerning         公司報告任何有關本集團之可疑不當行
the Group. The Company adopted the whistleblowing policy to             為、失當行為或瀆職行為。本公司採納
provide reporting channels and guidance on reporting possible           舉報政策，為舉報可能的不當行為提供
improprieties and reassurance to whistleblowers of the protection       舉報渠道及指引，並向舉報人保證本集
that the Group will extend to them in the formal system.                團將在正式系統中向彼等提供保護。

We will review the whistleblowing policy on a regular basis             我們將定期審閱舉報政策，並將任何可
and report any suspected cases to the legal and compliance              疑案例向本公司法律及合規部門匯報。
department of the Company.

 12 月31 日止年度確認的使用權資產減值虧損人民幣

     incurred a net loss of RMB983,562,000 and as of that date,      度，本集團產生淨虧損人民幣
     the Group had net current liabilities of RMB2,001,915,000.      983,562,000 元，且截至該日，
     In addition, bank and other borrowings of approximately         本集團的流動負債淨額為人民幣
     RMB206,100,000 were overdue during the year and                 2,001,915,000元。此外，年內有約
     the Group has subsequently settled approximately                人民幣 206,100,000 元的銀行及其
     RMB13,600,000 of the overdue bank and other borrowing.          他借款到期未償還，而本集團後續
     These conditions indicate the existence of a material           已償還其中的約人民幣13,600,000
     uncertainty which may cast significant doubt on the Group’s     元。該等情況表明存在重大不確定
     ability to continue as a going concern.                         性，可能會對本集團的持續經營能
                                                                     力構成重大疑慮。

     Notwithstanding the aforesaid conditions, the consolidated      儘管出現上述情況，合併財務報表

     as a going concern for the foreseeable future. The directors    慮下列計劃及措施後，董事認為本

3.   MATERIAL ACCOUNTING POLICIES                                          3. 重大會計政策
     Basis of consolidation                                                  綜合基準
     The consolidated financial statements include the financial             合併財務報表包括本公司及其附屬
     statements of the Company and its subsidiaries (collectively            公司（統稱「本集團」）截至 2025 年
     referred to as the “Group”) for the year ended 31 December              12月31日止年度的財務報表。附屬
     2025. A subsidiary is an entity (including a structured entity),        公司為本公司直接或間接控制的實
     directly or indirectly, controlled by the Company. Control              體（包括結構性實體）。當本集團對
     is achieved when the Group is exposed, or has rights, to                參與投資對象業務的浮動回報承擔
     variable returns from its involvement with the investee and             風險或享有權利以及能透過對投資
     has the ability to affect those returns through its power over          對象的權力（即本集團獲賦予現有能
     the investee (i.e., existing rights that give the Group the             力以主導投資對象相關活動的既存
     current ability to direct the relevant activities of the investee).     權利）影響該等回報時，即取得控制
                                                                             權。

     Generally, there is a presumption that a majority of voting             一般情況下，有一個推定，即多數
     rights results in control. When the Company has less than               投票權形成控制權。倘本公司擁有
     a majority of the voting or similar rights of an investee, the          少於投資對象大多數投票或類似權
     Group considers all relevant facts and circumstances in                 利的權利，則本集團於評估其是否
     assessing whether it has power over an investee, including:             擁有對投資對象的權力時會考慮一
                                                                             切相關事實及情況，包括：

     (a)   the contractual arrangement with the other vote                   (a)     與投資對象其他投票持有人的
           holders of the investee;                                                  合約安排；

     (b)   rights arising from other contractual arrangements; and           (b)     其他合約安排所產生的權利；
                                                                                     及

     (c)   the Group’s voting rights and potential voting rights.            (c)     本集團的投票權及潛在投票
                                                                                     權。

     The financial statements of the subsidiaries are prepared               附屬公司的財務報表乃於與本公司
     for the same reporting period as the Company, using                     相同的報告期內採納一致的會計政
     consistent accounting policies. The results of subsidiaries             策編製。附屬公司的業績乃於本集
     are consolidated from the date on which the Group obtains               團取得控制權的日期起綜合入賬，
     control, and continue to be consolidated until the date that            並繼續綜合入賬直至該控制權終止
     such control ceases.                                                    當日為止。

           noncontrolling interest and the exchange fluctuation reserve;     譽）、負債、任何非控股權益及匯兌

3.   MATERIAL ACCOUNTING POLICIES                                     3. 重大會計政策（續）
     (continued)
     Business combinations                                              業務合併
     Business combinations are accounted for using the                  業務合併按收購法列賬。轉讓代價
     acquisition method. The consideration transferred is               乃按收購日期之公允價值計量，該
     measured at the acquisition date fair value which is the sum       公允價值為本集團轉讓的資產、本
     of the acquisition date fair values of assets transferred by       集團自被收購方的前度擁有人承擔
     the Group, liabilities assumed by the Group to the former          的負債及本集團發行以換取被收購
     owners of the acquiree and the equity interests issued by          方控股權的股本權益於收購日期公
     the Group in exchange for control of the acquiree. For each        允價值的總和。就各項業務合併而
     business combination, the Group elects whether to measure          言，本集團選擇以公允價值或被收
     the non-controlling interests in the acquiree at fair value or     購方可識別資產淨值的應佔比例，
     at the proportionate share of the acquiree’s identifiable net      計算於被收購方之非控股權益。非
     assets. All other components of non-controlling interests          控股權益之一切其他部分乃按公允
     are measured at fair value. Acquisition-related costs are          價值計量。收購相關成本於產生時
     expensed as incurred.                                              列為開支。

      3.   MATERIAL ACCOUNTING POLICIES                                      3. 重大會計政策（續）
           (continued)
           Business combinations (continued)                                   業務合併（續）
           The Group determines that it has acquired a business                本集團決定當所取得的一組活動及
           when the acquired set of activities and assets includes an          資產包括一項投入及一項實質性過
           input and a substantive process that together significantly         程，而二者共同顯著促進創造產出
           contribute to the ability to create outputs.                        的能力時，其已收購一項業務。

           When the Group acquires a business, it assesses the                 本集團收購一項業務時會根據合約
           financial assets and liabilities assumed for appropriate            條款、收購日之經濟狀況及有關條
           classification and designation in accordance with the               件評估取得的金融資產及承擔的金
           contractual terms, economic circumstances and pertinent             融負債，以進行適當分類及指定。
           conditions as at the acquisition date. This includes the            這包括分離被收購方主合約中的嵌
           separation of embedded derivatives in host contracts of the         入式衍生工具。
           acquiree.


           If the business combination is achieved in stages, the              倘業務合併為分階段實現，先前持
           previously held equity interest is remeasured at its                有的股本權益應按收購日的公允價
           acquisition date fair value and any resulting gain or loss is       值重新計算，產生的任何收益或虧
           recognised in profit or loss.                                       損在損益中確認。

           Any contingent consideration to be transferred by the               收購方轉讓的任何或有代價將以收
           acquirer is recognised at fair value at the acquisition date.       購日的公允價值確認。被分類為資
           Contingent consideration classified as an asset or liability is     產或負債的或有代價按公允價值確
           measured at fair value with changes in fair value recognised        認，其公允價值的變化於損益確
           in profit or loss. Contingent consideration that is classified      認。或有代價如被分類為權益項
           as equity is not remeasured and subsequent settlement is            目，則不再對其重新計量，後續的
           accounted for within equity.                                        結算會計入權益中。

3.   MATERIAL ACCOUNTING POLICIES                                        3. 重大會計政策（續）
     (continued)
     Fair value measurement                                                公允價值計量
     The Group measures its investment properties, investment              本集團於各報告期末按公允價值計
     properties classified as held for sale, equity investments            量其投資物業、分類為持作出售的
     and biological assets at fair value at the end of each                投資物業、股本投資及生物資產。
     reporting period. Fair value is the price that would be               公允價值為市場參與者於計量日期
     received to sell an asset or paid to transfer a liability in          在有序交易中出售資產所收取的價
     an orderly transaction between market participants at the             格或轉讓負債所支付的價格。公允
     measurement date. The fair value measurement is based                 價值計量乃根據假設出售資產或轉
     on the presumption that the transaction to sell the asset or          讓負債的交易於資產或負債主要市
     transfer the liability takes place either in the principal market     場或（在無主要市場情況下）最具優
     for the asset or liability, or in the absence of a principal          勢市場進行而作出。主要或最具優
     market, in the most advantageous market for the asset or              勢市場須為本集團可進入之市場。
     liability. The principal or the most advantageous market              資產或負債的公允價值乃按假設市
     must be accessible by the Group. The fair value of an asset           場參與者於資產或負債定價時會以
     or a liability is measured using the assumptions that market          其最佳經濟利益行事計量。
     participants would use when pricing the asset or liability,
     assuming that market participants act in their economic best
     interest.


     A fair value measurement of a non-financial asset takes into          非金融資產的公允價值計量須計及
     account a market participant’s ability to generate economic           市場參與者能自最大限度使用該資
     benefits by using the asset in its highest and best use or by         產達致最佳用途，或將該資產出售
     selling it to another market participant that would use the           予將最大限度使用該資產達致最佳
     asset in its highest and best use.                                    用途的其他市場參與者，所產生的
                                                                           經濟效益。

     The Group uses valuation techniques that are appropriate in           本集團採納適用於不同情況且具備
     the circumstances and for which sufficient data are available         充分數據以供計量公允價值的估值
     to measure fair value, maximising the use of relevant                 方法，以盡量使用相關可觀察輸入
     observable inputs and minimising the use of unobservable              數據及盡量減少使用不可觀察輸入
     inputs.                                                               數據。

           measurement as a whole:

                                                                                     整）

                      or indirectly                                                  估值方法

                      lowest level input that is significant to the fair             屬重大的不可觀察最低層
                      value measurement is unobservable                              輸入數據的估值方法

3.   MATERIAL ACCOUNTING POLICIES                                      3. 重大會計政策（續）
     (continued)
     Impairment of non-financial assets                                  非金融資產減值
     Where an indication of impairment exists, or when annual            當 資 產（ 存 貨 、 遞 延 稅 項 資 產 、 投
     impairment testing for an asset is required (other than             資物業及分類為持作出售的投資物
     inventories, deferred tax assets, investment properties and         業除外）有減值跡象或須對資產進行
     investment properties classified as held for sale), the asset’s     年度減值測試時，會估計資產的可
     recoverable amount is estimated. An asset’s recoverable             收回金額。資產可收回金額指資產
     amount is the higher of the asset’s or cash-generating unit’s       或現金產生單位的使用價值與其已
     value in use and its fair value less costs of disposal, and is      扣除銷售成本的公允價值兩者之中
     determined for an individual asset, unless the asset does not       的較高者，基於個別資產釐定，除
     generate cash inflows that are largely independent of those         非該資產所產生的現金流基本上獨
     from other assets or groups of assets, in which case the            立於其他資產或資產組別的現金流
     recoverable amount is determined for the cash-generating            入，在該情況下可收回金額按資產
     unit to which the asset belongs. In testing a cash-generating       所屬現金產生單位釐定。對現金產
     unit for impairment, a portion of the carrying amount of a          生單位進行減值測試時，如可合理
     corporate asset (e.g., a headquarters building) is allocated        及貫徹進行分配，則公司資產（例如
     to an individual cash-generating unit if it can be allocated        總部大廈）的一部分賬面值分配至個
     on a reasonable and consistent basis or, otherwise, to the          別現金產生單位，否則分配至最小
     smallest group of cash-generating units.                            的現金產生單位組別。

     An impairment loss is recognised only if the carrying amount        僅當資產的賬面值超逾其可收回金
     of an asset exceeds its recoverable amount. In assessing            額時方會確認減值虧損。評估使用
     value in use, the estimated future cash flows are discounted        價值時，估計未來現金流量以反映
     to their present value using a pre-tax discount rate that           當前市場評定的貨幣時間價值以及
     reflects current market assessments of the time value of            該項資產特有風險的稅前折現率折
     money and the risks specific to the asset. An impairment            現至現值。減值虧損於產生期間在
     loss is charged to the statement of profit or loss in the           損益表中列入開支，惟倘資產按重
     period in which it arises, unless the asset is carried at a         估金額列賬，則減值虧損根據該重
     revalued amount, in which case the impairment loss is               估資產的有關會計政策入賬。
     accounted for in accordance with the relevant accounting
     policy for that revalued asset.

     owner-occupied property becomes an investment property,          的物業成為一項投資物業，本集團

           The Group’s business model for managing financial assets            本集團管理金融資產的業務模式，
           refers to how it manages its financial assets in order to           乃指其管理金融資產以產生現金流

           held within a business model with the objective to hold             為持有金融資產以收取合約現金流
           financial assets in order to collect contractual cash flows,        量的業務模式中持有，而透過其他
           while financial assets classified and measured at fair value        全面收益按公允價值分類及計量的
           through other comprehensive income are held within a                金融資產於一個目的同時為持有以

           contractual cash flows and selling. Financial assets which          式中持有。並非於上述業務模式中
           are not held within the aforementioned business models are          持有的金融資產透過損益按公允價
           classified and measured at fair value through profit or loss.       值分類及計量。

3.   MATERIAL ACCOUNTING POLICIES                                      3. 重大會計政策（續）
     (continued)
     Impairment of financial assets                                      金融資產減值
     The Group recognises an allowance for expected credit               本集團就所有並非透過損益按公允
     losses (“ECLs”) for all debt instruments not held at fair value     價值列賬持有的債務工具確認預期
     through profit or loss. ECLs are based on the difference            信貸虧損（「預期信貸虧損」）撥備。
     between the contractual cash flows due in accordance with           預期信貸虧損基於根據合約到期的
     the contract and all the cash flows that the Group expects          合約現金流量與本集團預期收取的
     to receive, discounted at an approximation of the original          所有現金流量（按原實際利率的約數
     effective interest rate. The expected cash flows will include       折現）之間的差額計算。預期現金流
     cash flows from the sale of collateral held or other credit         量將包括來自出售所持有屬於合約
     enhancements that are integral to the contractual terms.            條款整體部分的抵押品或其他信貸
                                                                         加強措施的現金流量。

     General approach                                                   一般方法
     ECLs are recognised in two stages. For credit exposures             預期信貸虧損分兩個階段確認。對
     for which there has not been a significant increase in credit       於自首次確認以來信貸風險並無大
     risk since initial recognition, ECLs are provided for credit        幅增加的信貸敞口而言，就因未來
     losses that result from default events that are possible within     12 個月內可能發生的違約事件而引
     the next 12 months (a 12-month ECL). For those credit               致的信貸虧損（一項 12 個月預期信
     exposures for which there has been a significant increase           貸虧損）計提預期信貸虧損撥備。對
     in credit risk since initial recognition, a loss allowance is       於自首次確認以來信貸風險已大幅
     required for credit losses expected over the remaining life         增加的信貸敞口而言，則須就預期
     of the exposure, irrespective of the timing of the default (a       於敞口餘下年期內的信貸虧損計提
     lifetime ECL).                                                      虧損撥備，而不論違約時間（一項存
                                                                         續期預期信貸虧損）。

     At each reporting date, the Group assesses whether                  於各報告日期，本集團評估金融工
     the credit risk on a financial instrument has increased             具的信貸風險自首次確認以來有否
     significantly since initial recognition. When making the            大幅增加。在作出該項評估時，本
     assessment, the Group compares the risk of a default                集團將金融工具於報告日期發生違
     occurring on the financial instrument as at the reporting           約的風險與金融工具於首次確認日
     date with the risk of a default occurring on the financial          期發生違約的風險作出比較，並考
     instrument as at the date of initial recognition and considers      慮在毋須付出不必要成本或努力下
     reasonable and supportable information that is available            可得的合理及有根據資料，包括過
     without undue cost or effort, including historical and              往及前瞻性資料。當合約付款逾期
     forward-looking information. The Group considers that               超過30 日時，本集團認為信貸風險
     there has been a significant increase in credit risk when           已大幅增加。
     contractual payments are more than 30 days past due.

     transaction costs.                                                    易成本。

           The subsequent measurement of financial liabilities depends          視乎金融負債的分類，金融負債的
           on their classification as follows:                                  後續計量方法如下：

           Financial liabilities at amortised cost (trade and other            按攤餘成本計算的金融負債（貿易及
           payables, and borrowings)                                           其他應付款項以及借貸）
           After initial recognition, trade and other payables, and             首次確認後，貿易及其他應付款項
           interest-bearing borrowings are subsequently measured                以及計息借貸其後以實際利率法按
           at amortised cost, using the effective interest rate method          攤餘成本計量，倘折讓影響不大，
           unless the effect of discounting would be immaterial, in             則按成本列賬。倘負債終止確認及
           which case they are stated at cost. Gains and losses are             已透過實際利率攤銷，則收入及虧
           recognised in the statement of profit or loss when the               損於損益表確認。
           liabilities are derecognised as well as through the effective
           interest rate amortisation process.


           Amortised cost is calculated by taking into account any              計算攤餘成本時已計及任何收購折
           discount or premium on acquisition and fees or costs                 讓或溢價，以及實際利率所包含的
           that are an integral part of the effective interest rate. The        費用或成本。實際利率攤銷計入損
           effective interest rate amortisation is included in finance          益表的財務成本。
           costs in the statement of profit or loss.


           Derecognition of financial liabilities                               終止確認金融負債
           A financial liability is derecognised when the obligation under      金融負債於負債責任解除、取消或
           the liability is discharged or cancelled, or expires.                屆滿時終止確認。

           When an existing financial liability is replaced by another          倘現有金融負債被另一項由同一借
           from the same lender on substantially different terms, or the        貸人提供而絕大部分條款不同的負

           an exchange or modification is treated as a derecognition of         修訂，則有關取代或修訂視作以終
           the original liability and a recognition of a new liability, and     止確認原有負債及確認新負債方式
           the difference between the respective carrying amounts is            處理，而相關賬面值的差額會於損
           recognised in the statement of profit or loss.                       益表內確認。

      3.   MATERIAL ACCOUNTING POLICIES                                      3. 重大會計政策（續）
           (continued)
           Government grants                                                   政府補貼
           Government grants are recognised at their fair value where          倘能合理確定將可收取政府補貼並
           there is reasonable assurance that the grant will be received       符合所有附帶條件，則按公允價值
           and all attaching conditions will be complied with. When the        確認政府補貼。倘補貼與開支項目
           grant relates to an expense item, it is recognised as income        有關，則會在擬補貼成本支銷的相
           on a systematic basis over the periods that the costs, for          應期間內有系統地確認補貼為收益。
           which it is intended to compensate, are expensed.


           Where the grant relates to an asset, the fair value is credited     倘補貼與資產有關，則公允價值計
           to a deferred income account and is released to the                 入遞延收入賬，並於有關資產估計
           statement of profit or loss over the expected useful life of        可使用年期按等額分期撥至損益
           the relevant asset by equal annual instalments or deducted          表，或自資產賬面值扣除並透過減
           from the carrying amount of the asset and released to the           少折舊開支的方式撥至損益表。
           statement of profit or loss by way of a reduced depreciation
           charge.


           Revenue recognition                                                 收入確認
           Revenue from contracts with customers                              來自客戶合約的收入
           Revenue from contracts with customers is recognised when            來自客戶合約的收入於貨品或服務
           control of goods or services is transferred to the customers        的控制權轉移至客戶時，按反映本
           at an amount that reflects the consideration to which the           集團預期於該等貨品或服務交易中
           Group expects to be entitled in exchange for those goods            有權收取的代價的金額確認。
           or services.


           When the consideration in a contract includes a variable            當合約中的代價有可變金額時，會
           amount, the amount of consideration is estimated to which           估計本集團就向客戶轉移貨品或服
           the Group will be entitled in exchange for transferring             務交易中將有權收取的代價金額。
           the goods or services to the customer. The variable                 可變代價於訂立合約時估計並以此
           consideration is estimated at contract inception and                為限，直至與可變代價相關的不確
           constrained until it is highly probable that a significant          定性其後獲解決時，已確認的累計
           revenue reversal in the amount of cumulative revenue                收入金額相當可能不會發生重大收
           recognised will not occur when the associated uncertainty           入撥回為止。
           with the variable consideration is subsequently resolved.

      4.   CRITICAL ACCOUNTING JUDGEMENTS                                    4. 重 大 會 計 判 斷 及 估 計 不 確
           AND KEY SOURCES OF ESTIMATION                                        定因素的主要來源
           UNCERTAINTY
           In the application of the Group’s accounting policies,               於 應 用 本 集 團 會 計 政 策（ 附 註 3 所
           which are described in Note 3, management is required to             述）時，管理層須就不可透過其他資
           make judgements, estimates and assumptions about the                 料來源而輕易取得的資產及負債賬
           carrying amounts of assets and liabilities that are not readily      面值作出判斷、估計及假設。該等
           apparent from other sources. The estimates and underlying            估計及相關假設乃根據過往經驗及
           assumptions are based on historical experience and other             其他被視為相關的因素作出。實際
           factors that are considered to be relevant. Actual results           結果可能有別於該等估計。
           may differ from these estimates.


           The estimates and underlying assumptions are reviewed on             將會持續對該等估計及相關假設作
           an ongoing basis. Revisions to accounting estimates are              出檢討。倘會計估計的修訂僅對作
           recognised in the period in which the estimate is revised            出修訂的期間產生影響，則有關修
           if the revision affects only that period or in the period of         訂只會在該期間內確認；倘會計估
           the revision and future periods if the revision affects both         計的修訂對現時及未來期間均產生
           current and future periods.                                          影響，則會在作出該修訂期間及未
                                                                                來期間內確認。

           have the most significant effect on the amounts recognised           最重大影響的判斷。
           in the consolidated financial statements.

     set out in Note 13.                                                估值法為基準計算。

            Sales of merchandise                                                  貨品銷售
            The performance obligation is satisfied upon                          當交付貨品且客戶一般須同時
            delivery of the goods and payment by the customer                     付款時，即滿足履約責任。並
            simultaneously is normally required. There is no right                無任何會引致代價可變的退貨
            of return or volume rebate which gives rise to variable               或大額回扣權利。
            consideration.


            Ticket sales                                                          門票銷售
            The performance obligation is satisfied over time as                  履約責任隨著提供服務的時間
            services are rendered and payment in advance is                       滿足，一般須預先付款，惟通
            normally required, except for tickets sold through travel             過旅行社出售的門票除外，其
            agencies, where payment is generally due within 30 to                 付款一般於交付門票起30 至
            90 days from the delivery of tickets.                                 90日到期。

            Provision of in-park recreation services                              提供園內遊樂服務
            The performance obligation is satisfied over time as                  履約責任隨著提供服務的時間
            services are rendered and payment by the customer                     滿足，一般客戶須於提供服務
            in advance before provision of services is normally                   前預先付款。
            required.


            Hotel operation                                                       酒店營運
            The performance obligation is satisfied over time as                  履約責任隨著提供服務的時間
            services are rendered and payment by the customer                     滿足，一般客戶須於提供服務
            in advance before provision of services is normally                   前預先付款。
            required.


            Tourism & leisure services and solutions                              文旅服務及解決方案
            The performance obligation is satisfied over time                     履約責任隨著提供服務的時間
            as services are rendered and short-term advances                      滿足，提供服務前一般須預付
            are normally required before rendering the services.                  短期款項。管理服務合約為期
            Management service contracts are for periods of one                   一年或以下，並根據合約條款
            year or less, and are billed based on the milestone                   按進度指標開具賬單。
            according to contract terms.

9.   DIRECTORS ’ AND CHIEF EXECUTIVE ’ S                         9. 董 事 及 行 政 總 裁 酬 金 及 五
     REMUNERATION AND FIVE HIGHEST PAID                             名最高薪酬僱員的酬金
     EMPLOYEES’ REMUNERATION
     Directors’ and chief executive’s remuneration for 2025         董事及行政總裁於 2025 年及 2024
     and 2024, disclosed pursuant to the Rules Governing the        年的酬金根據聯交所證券上市規
     Listing of Securities on the Stock Exchange (the “Listing      則（「上市規則」）、香港公司條例第

           discount rate of 13.5%. The discount rate used is pretax        13.5% 的折現率折現。所用折現率

   Below is a summary of the valuation technique used and the             以下為投資物業估值所用的估值方
   key inputs to the valuation of investment properties:                  法及主要輸入數據之概要：

                            Valuation             Significant                              Range or
                            technique             unobservable inputs                 weighted average
                            估值方法                  重大不可觀察輸入數據                           範圍或加權平均數
                                                                                         2025            2024
                                                                                       2025年           2024年


   Commercial properties    Income method         Market monthly rental
                                                  (RMB/sq.m. or RMB/space)              11-85           50-93
   商用物業                     收入法                   市場每月租金（人民幣元 ╱
                                                  平方米或人民幣元 ╱ 面積）
                                                  Term yield                       5.0%-6.0%       5.0%-6.0%
                                                  期限收益率
                                                  Market yield                     5.5%-6.5%       5.5%-6.5%
                                                  市場收益率

   Under the income method, fair value is estimated using                 根據收入法，公允價值乃採用有關
   assumptions regarding the benefits and liabilities of                  資產壽命內所有權收益及負債的假
   ownership over the asset’s life including an exit or terminal          設進行估計（包括退出價值或終端價
   value. This method involves the projection of a series of              值）。該方法涉及對物業權益的一連
   cash flows on a property interest. A market-derived discount           串現金流量的預測。市場衍生的折
   rate is applied to the projected cash flow in order to                 現率適用於預測現金流量以便確立
   establish the present value of the income stream associated            與資產有關的收入流的現值。
   with the asset.

           The duration of the cash flows and the specific timing of            現金流量的持續時間及流入額和流
           inflows and outflows are determined by events such as rent           出額的具體時間乃由諸如租金檢
           reviews, lease renewal and related reletting, redevelopment          討、租約續簽及相關續租、重建或
           or refurbishment. The appropriate duration is driven by              翻新等事件決定。適當的持續時間
           market behaviour that is a characteristic of the class of            受 市 場 行 為（ 為 物 業 類 別 的 一 個 特
           property. The periodic cash flow is estimated as income              性 ）影 響 。 定 期 現 金 流 量 按 收 入 扣
           less non-recoverable expenses, collection losses, lease              除不可回收費用、收賬損失、租賃
           incentives, maintenance costs, agent and commission costs            獎勵、維修費用、代理和佣金費用
           and other operating and management expenses. The series              及其他經營和管理費用估算。該一
           of periodic net operating income, along with an estimate of          連串定期經營收入淨額，連同預計
           the terminal value anticipated at the end of the projection          於預測期終結時之終端價值估計金
           period, is then discounted.                                          額，折現至現值。

           A significant increase/(decrease) in the market monthly rental       市場每月租金每年單獨大幅增加 ╱
           in isolation would result in a significant increase/(decrease)       （減少）將導致投資物業的公允價值
           in the fair value of the investment properties. A significant        大幅增加 ╱（減少）。折現率單獨大
           increase/(decrease) in the discount rate in isolation would          幅增加 ╱（減少）將導致投資物業的
           result in a significant decrease/(increase) in the fair value of     公允價值大幅減少 ╱（增加）。
           the investment properties.


      14. LEASES                                                              14. 租賃
           The Group as a lessee                                                本集團作為承租人

           The provision rates are based on ageing for groupings of           貸虧損。撥備率根據具有類似虧損
           various customer segments with similar loss patterns (i.e.,        模式的多個客戶群組別（即按客戶類
           by customer type). The calculation reflects the probability-       型劃分）的賬齡而釐定。該計算反映
           weighted outcome, the time value of money and reasonable           概率加權結果、貨幣時間價值及於
           and supportable information that is available at the reporting     報告日期可得有關過去事項、當前
           date about past events, current conditions and forecasts of        狀況及未來經濟狀況預測的合理及
           future economic conditions.                                        有根據資料。

32. RESERVES                                                      32. 儲備
   The amounts of the Group’s reserves and the movements            本集團於本年度及以往年度的儲備
   therein for the current and prior years are presented in the     金額及其變動已載於合併權益變動
   consolidated statement of changes in equity.                     表。


   (a) Capital reserve                                              (a) 資本儲備
        The amounts represent deemed contributions from                 該款項指視為權益持有人注資
        equity holders less deemed distributions to equity              減視為分派予權益持有人、本
        holders, considerations paid by the Group to non-               集團向非控股權益持有人就其
        controlling equity holders over their then share of net         當時分佔所收購附屬公司資產
        assets of subsidiaries acquired, and dilution upon              淨值支付代價及非控股權益持
        capital contributions by the non-controlling equity
                                                                        有人注資時的攤薄。
        holders.


   (b) Statutory reserves                                           (b) 法定儲備
        In accordance with the PRC Company Law and                      根據中國公司法及中國附屬公
        the PRC subsidiaries’ articles of association, each             司的章程細則，在中國註冊為
        subsidiary registered in the PRC as a domestic                  內資公司的附屬公司各自須撥
        company is required to appropriate 10% of its annual            出 10% 的年度法定純利（經抵
        statutory net profit as determined under generally              銷任何往年虧損後）至法定盈
        accepted accounting principles of The People’s                  餘儲備。年度法定純利按中華
        Republic of China (“PRC GAAP”) (after offsetting any            人民共和國公認會計準則（「中
        prior years’ losses) to the statutory surplus reserves.         國會計準則」）釐定。倘該儲備
        When the balance of this reserve fund reaches 50%               金結餘達該實體資本的50%，
        of the entity’s capital, any further appropriation              則可選擇作出任何額外撥款。
        is optional. The statutory reserves can be utilised
                                                                        法定儲備可用作抵銷往年虧損
                                                                        或增資。然而，作上述用途
        to offset prior years’ losses or to increase capital.
                                                                        後，該法定盈餘儲備結餘須維
        However, the balance of the statutory surplus reserves
                                                                        持為不少於資本的25%。
        must be maintained at a minimum of 25% of the
        capital after these usages.

      38. FAIR VALUE AND FAIR VALUE HIERARCHY                             38. 金 融 工 具 公 允 價 值 及 公 允
          OF FINANCIAL INSTRUMENTS (continued)                                價值等級（續）
           The Group’s finance department headed by the finance              以本集團財務經理為首的財務部門
           manager is responsible for determining the policies and           負責釐定金融工具公允價值計量的
           procedures for the fair value measurement of financial            政策及程序。財務經理直接向財務
           instruments. The finance manager reports directly to              總監及董事會報告。於各報告日
           the chief financial officer and the board of directors. At        期，財務部門分析金融工具的價值
           each reporting date, the finance department analyses              變動並釐定估值中適用的主要參
           the movements in the values of financial instruments and          數。估值由財務總監審核及批准。
           determines the major inputs applied in the valuation. The         估值過程及結果與董事會每年討論
           valuation is reviewed and approved by the chief financial         一次以進行年度財務申報。
           officer. The valuation process and results are discussed
           with the board of directors once a year for annual financial
           reporting.


           The fair values of the financial assets are included at the       計入金融資產的公允價值，乃自願
           amount at which the instrument could be exchanged in a            訂約方於目前交易（受脅迫或清盤出
           current transaction between willing parties, other than in        售交易除外）中可交換工具的金額。
           a forced or liquidation sale. The following methods and           估計公允價值時使用的方法及假設
           assumptions were used to estimate the fair values:                如下：

38. FAIR VALUE AND FAIR VALUE HIERARCHY                               38. 金 融 工 具 公 允 價 值 及 公 允
    OF FINANCIAL INSTRUMENTS (continued)                                  價值等級（續）
   The fair values of unlisted equity investments designated at          指定為透過損益按公允價值列賬
   fair value through profit or loss have been estimated using           的非上市股本投資的公允價值，
   a market-based valuation technique based on assumptions               乃採用市場估值方法基於並無可
   that are not supported by observable market prices or                 觀察市場價格或比率支持的假設
   rates. The valuation requires the directors to determine              估計得出。進行估值時，董事須
   comparable public companies (peers) based on industry,                根據行業、規模、槓桿及策略決
   size, leverage and strategy, and to calculate an appropriate          定 可 比 較 公 眾 公 司（ 同 業 ）， 以 及
   price multiple, such as enterprise value to earnings before           就每間已識別的可比較公司計算
   interest, taxes, depreciation and amortisation (“EV/EBITDA”)          相關價格倍數，例如企業價值相
   multiple and price to earnings (“P/E”) multiple, for each
                                                                         對未計利息、稅項、折舊及攤銷前
                                                                         盈利（「E V / E B I T D A」）倍數及市盈
   comparable company identified. The multiple is calculated
                                                                        （「P/E」）率。該倍數乃按可比較公
   by dividing the enterprise value of the comparable company
                                                                         司的企業價值除以盈利的計量方法
   by an earnings measure. The trading multiple is then
                                                                         計算得出。交易倍數因而就可比較
   discounted for considerations such as illiquidity and size
                                                                         公司基於公司特定事實及情況的流
   differences between the comparable companies based on
                                                                         動性不足及規模差異等考慮因素作
   company-specific facts and circumstances. The discounted
                                                                         出折讓。折讓後的倍數應用於非上
   multiple is applied to the corresponding earnings measure
                                                                         市股本投資的相關盈利計量，以計

      39. F I N A N C I A L R I S K M A N A G E M E N T                     39. 財務風險管理目標及政策
          OBJECTIVES AND POLICIES
           The Group’s principal financial instruments comprise               本集團的主要金融工具包括計息銀
           interest-bearing bank and other borrowings, financial              行及其他借款、透過損益按公允價
           assets at fair value through profit or loss, pledged deposits      值列賬的金融資產、已抵押存款以
           and cash and bank balances. The main purpose of these              及現金及銀行結餘。此等金融工具
           financial instruments is to raise finance for the Group’s          主要用於為本集團籌集經營資金。
           operations. The Group has various other financial assets and       本集團有直接來自其運營的多種其
           liabilities such as trade receivables, trade payables, other       他金融資產及負債，例如貿易應收
           receivables and other payables, which arise directly from its      款項、貿易應付款項、其他應收款
           operations.                                                        項及其他應付款項。


           It is, and has been throughout the year under review, the          本集團的政策為及於回顧年度一直
           Group’s policy that no trading in financial instruments shall      為不進行金融工具交易。
           be undertaken.


           The main risks arising from the Group’s financial instruments      本集團的金融工具產生的主要風險
           are interest rate risk, foreign currency risk, credit risk and     為利率風險、外匯風險、信貸風險
           liquidity risk. The board of directors reviews and agrees          及流動資金風險。董事會檢討及協
           policies for managing each of these risks and they are             議控制各類風險的政策，有關政策
           summarised below.                                                  概述於下文。

           The primary objectives of the Group’s capital management            本集團資本管理的首要目標是維護

           support its business and maximise equity holders’ value.            權益持有人帶來最大價值。

           The Group manages its capital structure and makes                   本集團根據經濟狀況變動及相關資
           adjustments to it in light of changes in economic conditions        產的風險特點管理及調整資本結
           and the risk characteristics of the underlying assets. To           構。為維持或調整資本結構，本集
           maintain or adjust the capital structure, the Group may             團或會調整派付予股東的股息、向
           adjust the dividend payment to shareholders, return capital         股東歸還資本或發行新股。本集
           to shareholders or issue new shares. The Group is not               團不受任何外部施加的資本要求
           subject to any externally imposed capital requirements. No          規限。於截至 2025 年 12 月 31 日及
           changes were made in the objectives, policies or processes          2024 年 12 月 31 日止年度，目標、
           during the years ended 31 December 2025 and 31                      政策或程序並無任何變動。
           December 2024.

40. RETIREMENT BENEFIT SCHEMES                                    40. 退休福利計劃
   The Group operates a defined contribution MPF retirement         本集團根據香港強制性公積金計劃
   benefit scheme (the “MPF Scheme”) under the Hong Kong            條例運作一項界定供款強積金退休
   Mandatory Provident Fund Schemes Ordinance. Under the            福利計劃（「強積金計劃」）。根據強
   MPF Scheme, employees are required to contribute 5%              積金計劃，僱員須按每月薪金的5%
   of their monthly salaries or up to a maximum of HK$1,500         或最高1,500 港元（2024 年：1,500
   (2024: HK$1,500) and they can choose to make additional          港 元 ）供 款 ， 並 可 選 擇 作 出 額 外 供
   contributions. Employers’ monthly contributions are              款。僱主的每月供款則按僱員每月
   calculated at 5% of the employee’s monthly salaries or up to     薪金的5%或最多1,500 港元（2024
   a maximum of HK$1,500 (2024: HK$1,500) (the “Mandatory           年 ： 1 , 5 0 0 港 元 ）計 算（「 強 制 性 供
   Contributions”). Employees are entitled to 100% of the           款」）。僱員於65 歲退休、身故或完
   employer’s Mandatory Contributions upon their retirement         全喪失行為能力時，可獲僱主100%
   at the age of 65, death or total incapacity. The retirement      的強制性供款。已於合併損益及其
   benefit scheme contributions arising from the MPF Scheme         他全面收益表扣除的強積金計劃所
   charged to the consolidated statement of profit or loss and      產生退休福利計劃供款，指本集團
   other comprehensive income represent contributions paid or       按計劃規則所訂明的比率向基金已
   payable to the funds by the Group at rates specified in the      付或應付的供款。
   rules of the schemes.


   Employees of the Group’s subsidiaries in the PRC are             本集團於中國附屬公司的僱員均為
   members of a state-managed retirement benefit scheme             中國政府營運的國家管理退休福利
   operated by the PRC government. The Group is required            計劃的成員。本集團須向該退休福
   to contribute a specified percentage of payroll costs as         利計劃供款，供款額按各地方政府
   determined by respective local government authority to the       機關釐定的薪金成本指定百分比計
   retirement benefit scheme to fund the benefit scheme.            算，以為該福利計劃提供資金。

   At 31 December 2025 and 2024, there was no forfeited             於2025 年及2024 年12 月31 日，並
   contribution under any defined contribution schemes              無任何界定供款計劃項下已沒收供
   available which may be used by the Group to reduce the           款可讓本集團用作減少現有供款水
   existing level of contributions, nor any contribution under      平，而本集團於該兩個年度亦無沒
   any defined contribution schemes was forfeited by the            收任何界定供款計劃項下的任何供
   Group for both years. The contributions paid and payable         款。本集團已付及應付計劃供款於
   to the schemes by the Group are disclosed in Note 7 to the       合併財務報表附註7披露。
